# Complete Current Data Dictionary

Actual DEV snapshot: 2026-10-14T16:59:48.154042+00:00. 32 tables / 494 table columns; 4 views / 85 outputs.

Unknown quantities are not zero. No default declared differs from an explicit SQL NULL default. Keys below are actual declarations; enforcement is recorded in Declared_Constraints.csv, not inferred from a diagram. Technical source facts do not approve units or formulas.

The 13 October manual remains evidence for unchanged business explanations, not current counts. Actual metadata here supersedes its structural inventories. New technical workflows are explained in Documentation_Update_Handoff.md.

| AUDIT_ID | TEXT(36) | NO | No default declared | PK:PK_AUDIT_EVENT |
| ACTOR_USER_ID | TEXT(36) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ACTOR_EMAIL | TEXT(320) | NO | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ACTION | TEXT(100) | NO | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ENTITY_TYPE | TEXT(100) | NO | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ENTITY_ID | TEXT(1000) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| DETAILS | VARIANT | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| OCCURRED_AT | TIMESTAMP_TZ | NO | CONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9))) | No declared key membership |

## CORE.CONTACT
One contact identity, optionally linked to an Account.

| Field | Type | Nullable | Default | Declared keys |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CONTACT_ID | TEXT(36) | NO | No default declared | PK:PK_CONTACT |
| ACCOUNT_ID | TEXT(36) | YES | No default declared | FK:FK_CONTACT_ACCOUNT -> CORE.ACCOUNT.ACCOUNT_ID |
| FULL_NAME | TEXT(500) | NO | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| FIRST_NAME | TEXT(255) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| MIDDLE_NAME | TEXT(255) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| LAST_NAME | TEXT(255) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| JOB_TITLE | TEXT(255) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| EMAIL | TEXT(500) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| PHONE | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| MOBILE_PHONE | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| FAX | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ADDRESS_LINE1 | TEXT(500) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ADDRESS_LINE2 | TEXT(500) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| CITY | TEXT(255) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| PROVINCE | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| POSTAL_CODE | TEXT(50) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| COUNTRY_REGION | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| EIMS_CONTACT_NUMBER | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| LANGUAGE_PREFERENCE | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| PRIME_CONTACT | BOOLEAN | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| STATUS | TEXT(100) | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| CREATED_AT | TIMESTAMP_NTZ | YES | CAST(CONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9))) AS TIMESTAMP_NTZ(9)) | No declared key membership |
| UPDATED_AT | TIMESTAMP_NTZ | YES | CAST(CONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9))) AS TIMESTAMP_NTZ(9)) | No declared key membership |

| Field | Definition | Source / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| CONTACT_ID | Source Dataverse GUID retained without name-based replacement for one contact identity, optionally linked to an Account. Use the full declared key, not a display name. | Historical v3: contact.csv.contactid -> CONTACT_ID; converter string.; E05 data/migration.py:215-233 (_source_value); E05 data/migration.py:142-179 (_convert) |
| ACCOUNT_ID | Account associated with this contact record; references CORE.ACCOUNT.ACCOUNT_ID. This role must not be replaced by another similarly named reference. | Historical v3: contact.csv.parentcustomerid -> ACCOUNT_ID; converter string.; E05 data/migration.py:215-233 (_source_value); E05 data/migration.py:142-179 (_convert) |
| FULL_NAME | Stored contact display name; CONTACT is a list, separate from Account primary contact fields. | Historical v3: contact.csv.fullname -> FULL_NAME; converter string.; E05 data/migration.py:215-233 (_source_value); E05 data/migration.py:142-179 (_convert) |
| FIRST_NAME | Contact given-name component retained separately from the full display name. | Historical v3: contact.csv.firstname -> FIRST_NAME; converter string.; E05 data/migration.py:215-233 (_source_value); E05 data/migration.py:142-179 (_convert) |
| MIDDLE_NAME | Contact middle-name component when supplied; absence is not an invalid contact identity. | Historical v3: contact.csv.middlename -> MIDDLE_NAME; converter string.; E05 data/migration.py:215-233 (_source_value); E05 data/migration.py:142-179 (_convert) |

### Definitions and Sources

| Field | Definition | Source / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| EVENT_ID | Stable Flock event identity derived from Flock ID and request ID; repeated identical requests do not create another event. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| FLOCK_ID | Existing Flock whose whole-position or actual-end event is recorded; multiple events preserve its lifecycle history. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| EVENT_TYPE | MOVE or END. END denotes an asserted actual lifecycle end; estimated dates never create it. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| FROM_FACILITY_ID | Recorded origin for a staff-asserted move; corrections retain the origin of the superseded event. For END, the last recorded reference is retained, not inferred past occupancy. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| TO_FACILITY_ID | Destination of a whole-Flock MOVE; NULL for END. No partial-transfer quantity formula is asserted. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| EFFECTIVE_AT | Explicit timezone-aware business timestamp converted to UTC-naive storage. Unknown past movements are not invented. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| RECORDED_AT | UTC-naive system recording time in the new writer; distinct from the effective business time. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| RECORDED_BY | Authenticated initiating application user or explicit SYSTEM actor. This is not a declared FK to SECURITY.APP_USER. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| REASON | Operator explanation of the event/correction, not a generic replacement for business activity semantics. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| SUPERSEDES_EVENT_ID | Optional event being corrected, within the same Flock; the original event is retained. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| BEFORE_DATA | JSON snapshot before the recorded operation; new evidence encoding preserves dates as ISO text and blank cells as JSON null. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |
| AFTER_DATA | JSON snapshot after the operation; movement assertion metadata is explicit and does not fabricate original source values. | record_flock_event: explicit operator request, reason, timezone-aware business time; no generated past movement.; data/repositories/continuity.py; data/production_intake.py; data/producer_identity.py; data/repositories/snowflake.py |

## SECURITY.APP_USER
One application user/security identity.

| Field | Type | Nullable | Default | Declared keys |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| USER_ID | TEXT(36) | NO | No default declared | PK:PK_APP_USER |
| EMAIL | TEXT(320) | NO | No default declared | UNIQUE:UQ_APP_USER_EMAIL |
| DISPLAY_NAME | TEXT(255) | NO | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ROLE | TEXT(50) | NO | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| ACTIVE | BOOLEAN | NO | TRUE | No declared key membership |
| LAST_LOGIN_AT | TIMESTAMP_TZ | YES | No default declared | No declared key membership |
| CREATED_AT | TIMESTAMP_TZ | NO | CONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9))) | No declared key membership |
| UPDATED_AT | TIMESTAMP_TZ | NO | CONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9))) | No declared key membership |
